# 2화 컷 이미지 프롬프트 — 정밀 셀 반실사 「소리의 색」

한 컷씩 붙여 넣는다. 참고 이미지(renders/characters·props·sets)를 함께 올리면 일관성이 오른다. 결과는 storyboard/renders/cel/<컷번호>.png로 저장한다. 말풍선·효과음은 이미지에 넣지 않는다.

## 2-001 — S1 국밥집 천막 (낮, 1987년 9월~10월 주말들)
- 크기 800×1600 · MS · 아이레벨 · 동민(중), 선영(좌)
- 화면(한국어): 가로 2분할. 위: 검은 화면이다. 위쪽에 타이틀과 자막을 얹고, 선영의 또박또박 세는 목소리 말풍선만 하얗게 뜬다. 아래(F.I.): 국밥집 천막 안 평상 앞. 페인트 통 북(뼈다귀 북)을 끌어안고 숟가락 북채를 쳐든 채 굳은 동민의 얼굴을 정면 고정으로 잡는다. 왼쪽 끝에서 한 치수 큰 검정 장화를 신은 선영이 다급하게 몸을 기울이고(말풍선 좌상단), 동민은 눈썹을 올려 억울한 얼굴을 한다.

```
[STYLE] clean uniform dark-brown 2px outlines, two-tone cel shading, semi-realistic proportions, soft gradient only in sky and backlight, no paper texture, no film grain, vertical webtoon panel, panel 800x1600 (tall)
[SCENE] [main] grandma Sunrye's rice-soup tent at the island entrance: a patched canvas tent, a huge iron soup pot on a coal fire, low wooden sitting platforms (pyeongsang) with long low tables on them, a ladle, 1987 autumn weekends, afternoon, soft light through the canvas, key color #D9822B, cel shadows #6E5640; [1] the narrow earthen embankment road leading into the island, reeds on both sides, a cluster of shacks at the island entrance, Saturday morning, low sun from the left (east); [2] a mainland back alley of 1980s Seoul outside the island: low brick and cement-block houses, gray walls, wooden utility poles, a narrow lane of cracked asphalt and dirt, early afternoon with the sun still high; [5] the narrow earthen embankment road leading into the island, reeds on both sides, a cluster of shacks at the island entrance, afternoon; [8] a fictional landfill island on the Han River at the edge of Seoul, two flat-topped mesa-like hills of compacted trash still being built ('the big hill' and 'the small hill'), terraced ochre and gray-brown slopes with dump-truck ramps, specks of white plastic, power transmission towers in front, a single earthen embankment road linking it to the mainland, reeds along the water, hazy beige sky, October afternoon, cool clear light, river wind lifting the tent flaps; [10][11] on the small flat-topped hill of trash: terraced slope of compacted ochre soil mixed with scrap metal, broken boards, white plastic sheets and tin cans, a dump-truck ramp cut into the slope, the Han River and the 1980s city skyline far behind, daytime
[SET] Seen from the front entrance flap looking in (facing north): a long tent about 5 m wide and 8 m deep on a beaten-earth floor, patched beige and khaki canvas on a wooden pole frame, one tall wooden center pole halfway in with a bare bulb on a cord and a paper calendar nailed to it; at the far back the huge iron soup pot steaming on a brick coal fire with a small worktable and stacked bowls beside it, and to the right of the pot a rear flap that can be tied open; the benches are wide low wooden platforms (pyeongsang) with long low tables on them: two along the left (west) side and one long one along the right (east) side; the right side wall flap is rolled up, showing the corrugated-iron junk shop 3 m away; an open earth floor down the middle in front of the platforms. Camera position: from the west platforms toward the players standing on the middle floor (east). (keep the layout consistent with the reference image renders/sets/soup_tent_in.png)
[CHAR] Dongmin, 9-year-old Korean boy, 125cm, round face, very short uneven clipper buzz cut (same length all over, no fade), upper-left front tooth missing, big ears, adhesive bandage on the right knee; outfit in this scene: an oversized hand-me-down loose green cotton-knit tracksuit with a single contrasting stripe down the sides, baggy at the knees, elastic cuffs, no logo
[CHAR] Seonyoung, 23-year-old Korean woman, 162cm, slim, round face, voluminous softly permed 1980s bob with fluffy bangs, large round thin gold-rim glasses; outfit in this scene: a wrinkled white shirt, a cardigan, a long flared skirt
[PROP] 'the bone drum', a homemade drum made from a paint can and an X-ray film, a round steel paint pail slightly tapered toward the bottom with a wire bail handle (match the reference image renders/props/bone_drum__s2_complete.png)
[PROP] a pair of wooden spoons used as drumsticks, two plain wooden eating spoons with oval bowls, held by the handles, the round backs of the bowls used to strike, pale wood, each 20cm long (match the reference image renders/props/drumstick_spoons.png)
[PROP] a conductor's baton carved from a wooden spoon handle, a straight stick tapering to a fine tip, the grip end flat and wider where the spoon bowl was cut off, pale wood, hand-carved and sanded smooth (match the reference image renders/props/baton__s1.png)
[PROP] a pair of oversized black rubber boots, large men's knee-high black rubber boots, very wide shaft, shown standing next to a small pair of women's shoes for scale, black rubber, 38cm tall (match the reference image renders/props/big_black_boots.png)
[CUT] panel split horizontally into two frames. Top: a pure black empty frame. Bottom: medium shot, static camera, eye level, front view: Dongmin in the center hugging a paint-can drum, two wooden spoons raised, frozen mid-strike, eyebrows raised in an aggrieved look; Seonyoung at the left edge in oversized black rubber boots leaning in urgently with the spoon-handle baton. Soft diffused afternoon light through the tent canvas.
[NEG] no text, no letters, no speech bubbles, no brand logos, no real banknotes, no official Olympic mascot or emblem, fictional characters not resembling any real person, children drawn as ordinary kids, no injuries or blood
```

  - [caption] 자막: 1987년 가을

  - [sfx] SFX: 쿠웅따악!
  - [dialogue] 선영: 동민 씨, 셋이요, 셋!

## 2-002 — S1 국밥집 천막 (낮, 1987년 9월~10월 주말들)
- 크기 800×1400 · LS · 아이레벨 · 선영(중), 만석(중)
- 화면(한국어): 몽타주 1·2. 가로 2분할. 위: 토요일 아침 둑길이다. 동쪽 낮은 해가 화면 왼쪽에 있다. 선영이 빈 바이올린 케이스를 가슴에 안고 큰 검정 장화로 진흙을 철벅이며 건너온다. 아래: 해가 아직 높은 뭍의 골목. 만석이 등을 조금 굽히고 빈 리어카를 끌고 간다. 골목 끝에서 천막 쪽 엉망인 합주가 희미하게 섞였다가 사라진다(효과음 글자를 작고 흐리게).

## 2-003 — S1 국밥집 천막 (낮, 1987년 9월~10월 주말들)
- 크기 800×1400 · MS · 아이레벨 · 은주(좌), 미자(우), 덕수(중·후경), 동민(중·후경), 선영(전경·뒷모습)
- 화면(한국어): 몽타주 3. 가로 2분할. 위(코미디 데포르메 2~3등신): 선영의 지휘봉이 올라가자 악기 네 소리가 네 갈래로 튀어 흩어진다. 미자의 배수관 소리는 너무 크고 덕수의 기름통 소리는 한 박자 늦다. 아래(정상 비율): 은주가 동그리를 턱에 댄 채 고개를 왼쪽으로 기울이고 반 박자 쉰 얼굴로 미자를 본다. 미자는 배수관을 치켜들고 턱을 내민다(말풍선 우측).

## 2-004 — S1 국밥집 천막 (낮, 1987년 9월~10월 주말들)

## 2-005 — S1 국밥집 천막 (낮, 1987년 9월~10월 주말들)
- 크기 800×1600 · BS · 아이레벨 · 은주(좌), 덕수(우), 동민·미자(후경)
- 화면(한국어): 가로 2분할. 위: 은주가 담담하게 미자 쪽을 보며 말한다(입꼬리 움직임 없음). 옆의 덕수가 누룽지를 씹다가 풉, 웃음이 터져 부스러기가 튄다. 아래(몽타주 4, 코미디 데포르메 2~3등신): 구월 어느 오후. 덕수가 뚱보에 활을 긋자 줄이 끊어져 덕수 몸 반대쪽 허공으로 채찍처럼 휜다(아무도 맞지 않는다). 덕수가 뚱보를 끌어안은 채 뒤로 벌렁 넘어간다. 들여다보는 아이들 얼굴들 사이로 부감, 누운 덕수 입에 누룽지가 그대로 물려 있다.

```
[STYLE] clean uniform dark-brown 2px outlines, two-tone cel shading, semi-realistic proportions, soft gradient only in sky and backlight, no paper texture, no film grain, vertical webtoon panel, panel 800x1600 (tall)
[SCENE] [main] grandma Sunrye's rice-soup tent at the island entrance: a patched canvas tent, a huge iron soup pot on a coal fire, low wooden sitting platforms (pyeongsang) with long low tables on them, a ladle, 1987 autumn weekends, afternoon, soft light through the canvas, key color #D9822B, cel shadows #6E5640; [1] the narrow earthen embankment road leading into the island, reeds on both sides, a cluster of shacks at the island entrance, Saturday morning, low sun from the left (east); [2] a mainland back alley of 1980s Seoul outside the island: low brick and cement-block houses, gray walls, wooden utility poles, a narrow lane of cracked asphalt and dirt, early afternoon with the sun still high; [5] the narrow earthen embankment road leading into the island, reeds on both sides, a cluster of shacks at the island entrance, afternoon; [8] a fictional landfill island on the Han River at the edge of Seoul, two flat-topped mesa-like hills of compacted trash still being built ('the big hill' and 'the small hill'), terraced ochre and gray-brown slopes with dump-truck ramps, specks of white plastic, power transmission towers in front, a single earthen embankment road linking it to the mainland, reeds along the water, hazy beige sky, October afternoon, cool clear light, river wind lifting the tent flaps; [10][11] on the small flat-topped hill of trash: terraced slope of compacted ochre soil mixed with scrap metal, broken boards, white plastic sheets and tin cans, a dump-truck ramp cut into the slope, the Han River and the 1980s city skyline far behind, daytime
[SET] Seen from the front entrance flap looking in (facing north): a long tent about 5 m wide and 8 m deep on a beaten-earth floor, patched beige and khaki canvas on a wooden pole frame, one tall wooden center pole halfway in with a bare bulb on a cord and a paper calendar nailed to it; at the far back the huge iron soup pot steaming on a brick coal fire with a small worktable and stacked bowls beside it, and to the right of the pot a rear flap that can be tied open; the benches are wide low wooden platforms (pyeongsang) with long low tables on them: two along the left (west) side and one long one along the right (east) side; the right side wall flap is rolled up, showing the corrugated-iron junk shop 3 m away; an open earth floor down the middle in front of the platforms. (keep the layout consistent with the reference image renders/sets/soup_tent_in.png)
[CHAR] Eunju, 13-year-old Korean girl, 140cm, small and thin, sun-tanned skin, narrow small face, large long almond-shaped eyes, thick straight eyebrows, no bangs with the forehead visible, loose wisps of hair falling at both temples, faint freckles on nose and cheeks, long dark-brown hair tied low at the nape with a single rubber band, tilts her head slightly to the left when listening, a small silver tuning-fork pendant on a thin chain; outfit in this scene: an oversized navy work jacket (hand-me-down), worn trousers with frayed knees, rubber boots, bare hands (the white cotton gloves stuffed in the jacket pocket)
[CHAR] Deoksu, 12-year-old Korean boy, 155cm, big and chubby, round face, short crew cut (no fade), droopy gentle eyes, shirt buttons always done up one hole off; outfit in this scene: a blue plaid shirt over a stretched, slightly yellowed thin white cotton-knit sleeveless undershirt (a Korean 'running shirt', not an athletic tank top)
[CHAR] Dongmin, 9-year-old Korean boy, 125cm, round face, very short uneven clipper buzz cut (same length all over, no fade), upper-left front tooth missing, big ears, adhesive bandage on the right knee; outfit in this scene: an oversized hand-me-down loose green cotton-knit tracksuit with a single contrasting stripe down the sides, baggy at the knees, elastic cuffs, no logo
[CHAR] Mija, 14-year-old Korean girl, 158cm, tall for her age, broad shoulders, choppy self-cut short bob with bangs sticking out in all directions, thick eyebrows, square jaw, an adhesive bandage across the nose; outfit in this scene: a loose-fitting red cotton-knit tracksuit top with a single contrasting stripe down each sleeve, zip front, no logo, dark trousers
[PROP] 'Donggeuri', a homemade violin made from a milk-powder tin can, a large cylindrical milk-powder tin can with its paper label removed, laid on its side lengthwise as the body (15cm diameter (match the reference image renders/props/dongguri__s1_first.png)
[PROP] 'Ttungbo', a homemade cello made from half of a steel oil drum, the bottom half of a steel oil drum cut crosswise and stood upright with its closed round base facing forward as the soundboard (match the reference image renders/props/ttungbo__s2_complete.png)
[PROP] a piece of scorched rice crust, a flat irregular slab of crispy toasted rice with grains visible, toasted rice, 10 by 7cm, browned edges, crumbs (match the reference image renders/props/nurungji.png)
[PROP] a low wooden platform bench (pyeongsang), a wide low platform of parallel wooden planks on square legs, wood, 180 by 120cm, 45cm high, gaps between planks, soup stains, worn corners (match the reference image renders/props/pyeongsang.png)
[CUT] panel split horizontally into two frames. Top: bust shot, eye level: Eunju on the left speaking calmly, mouth corners not moving; Deoksu on the right bursting into a snort while chewing scorched rice, crumbs flying. Bottom: chibi comedic reaction, 2-3 heads tall, high angle looking down: Deoksu lying flat on his back on the low platform hugging the oil-can cello, a snapped string whipping away into the air on the side away from him, a scorched-rice cracker still in his mouth, kids' faces peering down at the frame edge.

## 2-006 — S1 국밥집 천막 (낮, 1987년 9월~10월 주말들)
- 크기 800×1400 · LS · 아이레벨 · 곽 영감(중), 섬 아저씨들(좌·우)

## 2-007 — S1 국밥집 천막 (낮, 1987년 9월~10월 주말들)
- 크기 800×1600 · MS · 아이레벨 · 선영(좌), 순례 할머니(우), 동민·미자(후경)

## 2-008 — S1 국밥집 천막 (낮, 1987년 9월~10월 주말들)

## 2-009 — S2 국밥집 천막 (오후, 1987년 10월 둘째 토요일)
- 크기 800×1600 · FS · 아이레벨 · 선영(좌·전경), 은주(중), 미자(중), 덕수(우), 동민(우)
- 화면(한국어): 가로 2분할. 위: 천막 기둥 전구 아래 평상 앞, 다섯 사람이 한 프레임에 있다. 선영의 지휘봉 끝이 세 번 까딱인다. 네 소리가 한 점으로 떨어지는 순간, 모두 악기에 손을 댄 채 화면이 정지한 듯 굳는다(효과음 글자가 한가운데 한 점으로 모인다). 아래: 흔들리는 전구, 고정. 전구 줄이 삐걱이고 따뜻한 빛이 천막 천 위를 오간다. 그 밖에는 아무 소리도 없다(침묵 3초, 빈 여백).

```

## 2-011 — S4 한빛문화회관 연습실 (낮)

- 화면(한국어): 가로 2분할. 위(INS): 버스 토큰을 쥔 선영의 손과 무릎 위 안내문. 안내문 글자는 읽히지 않는 줄로 그린다. 아래: 넓은 연습실 와이드. 반들반들한 나무 바닥, 하얀 형광등, 벽에 올림픽 표어 포스터(표어만)가 있다. 맨 앞에서 감색 재킷의 다솜중 오케스트라가 폭풍처럼 연주하고, 맨 앞자리 태준의 활이 오르면 뒤의 활들이 따라 오른다. 맨 뒷줄의 섬 아이들은 자루를 무릎에 끌어안았다. 선영이 몸을 기울여 떨리는 목소리로 속삭인다(작은 말풍선, 좌측). 음악에는 아직 색을 주지 않는다.

- 화면(한국어): 가로 2분할. 위: 의자에 앉은 채 고개만 돌려 동그리를 보는 태준. 정중하지만 차가운 눈이고, 얇은 입술을 다물었다. 전경에 은주의 어깨와 동그리가 흐릿하다. 아래: 선영이 지휘봉을 든다. 떨리는 지휘봉 끝 극접사, 목이 잠긴 입.

```

[SCENE] the fictional Hanbit Cultural Center, its large rehearsal room: a polished wooden floor, rows of folding chairs grouped by school, white fluorescent ceiling lights, tall windows, a slogan-only Olympic campaign poster on the wall, 1987 late October, daytime, flat white fluorescent light humming overhead plus a band of sunlight through the tall windows, warm indoors, key color #3A5A8C, cel shadows #2C3A55
[SET] Seen from the back of the room by the entrance door looking toward the front: an open playing area at the front of the room; rows of folding chairs facing it, grouped by school with gaps between the groups; tall windows along the left wall letting in bands of sunlight; the poster on the right wall; the last row of chairs right in front of the viewer. - 화면(한국어): 동민이 또 '둘'에서 터지고, 놀란 미자가 꽥꽥이를 힘껏 불고, 뚱보가 한 박자 늦게 따라온다. 동그리 자리만 효과음 글자가 비어 있다. 연습실이 웃음으로 무너지고, 다른 학교 아이 하나는 의자에서 미끄러진다. 전경 오른쪽의 태준만 웃지 않는다. 헝겊으로 바이올린 줄의 송진 가루를 닦으며, 닦던 손을 멈추지 않고 크지 않게 말한다. 웃음 글자는 크고 회갈색, 태준 말풍선은 작지만 또렷하다.

## 2-017 — S4 한빛문화회관 연습실 (낮)
- 크기 800×1600 · CU · 하이 · 은주(손·중), 태준(우·전경)
- 화면(한국어): 가로 2분할. 위: 은주의 손 극접사. 아무 말 없이 목에서 은색 소리굽쇠를 꺼내 무릎에 톡 치고 깡통 몸통에 댄다. 소리굽쇠에서 은빛 흰색 동심원이 퍼진다. 둘째 줄 나무못을 돌리자 줄의 떨림과 엇갈리던 물결이 하나로 포개진다. 아래: 태준 쪽에서 본 은주. 소리굽쇠를 옷 속에 넣고 귀로만 나머지 줄을 맞춘 은주가 활로 두 줄을 함께 긋는다. 깡통 안에서 두 소리가 떨림 없이 한 덩어리 금빛으로 울린다. 전경의 태준 헝겊이 멈추고, 웃음 글자는 낮게 가라앉아 회색으로 흐려진다. 말풍선 없음.

```

[CHAR] Taejun, 14-year-old Korean boy, 165cm, tall and slim, long face, narrow long eyes, sharp 7:3 side part with the forehead visible, thin lips, pale skin, very straight posture; outfit in this scene: a late-1980s Korean private middle-school uniform: a navy wool blazer with a small school badge, a white shirt, a plain dark tie, matching dark trousers in a loose straight cut; not a black high-collar uniform, not a sailor uniform

- 화면(한국어): 가로 2분할. 위: 손수건을 쥔 채 서 있는 최 계장. 관자놀이로 땀 한 줄기가 흐르지만 닦지 않는다. 아래: 최 계장이 고개를 들어 옆집 고물상의 함석지붕을 본다. 스스로도 왜 보는지 모르는 얼굴이다. 시선 끝에 함석지붕이 있고, 그 위로 구름 한 점 없는 맑은 하늘이 한 줄 보인다.

```

Camera position: from in front of the tent looking east up at the junk shop roof. (keep the layout consistent with the reference image renders/sets/soup_tent_front.png)

## 2-028 — S8 만복전당포 (아침, 1987년 11월 중순)
- 크기 800×1600 · LS · 하이 · 선영(중), 전당포 주인(우·후경)
- 화면(한국어): 가로 2분할. 위: 서리가 하얀 시장 골목 끝. 낡은 간판에서 문 앞 선영으로 내려온다. 간판의 '복' 자는 반쯤 떨어져 매달렸다. 선영이 숨을 한 번 고르고 안경을 고쳐 쓴다. 아래: 좁은 가게 안, 누렇게 깜빡이는 형광등. 유리 진열장에 손목시계·금반지·사진기가 누워 있다. 선영이 진열장 위에 빈 바이올린 케이스를 열어 놓았다. 붉은 벨벳 위에 악보와 사탕 봉지가 있고, 바이올린 자리는 비었다. 선영이 구겨진 지폐를 한 장씩 편다. 돋보기를 쓴 주인이 주판을 튕긴다.

```

[SCENE] a narrow 1980s pawnshop: glass display cases with watches, gold rings and cameras, a yellowed fluorescent light, an abacus on the counter, 1987 mid-November, frosty morning, inside a yellowed flickering fluorescent light, cold pale morning light at the door; exterior: a 1980s Seoul market alley: low shop fronts, an electronics repair shop with a radio, hand-painted fictional signs, white frost at the end of the alley, key color #B9A86A, cel shadows #5E5238

Camera position: from inside the door toward the counter. (keep the layout consistent with the reference image renders/sets/pawnshop.png)
[SET-2] for the other frame of the split panel: a 1980s Seoul market alley: low shop fronts, an electronics repair shop with a radio, hand-painted fictional signs. Seen from the mouth of the alley looking in: a narrow alley about 3 m wide lined with low one- and two-story shop fronts with blank hand-painted signboards and rolled-up awnings; on the near right, the electronics repair shop with radios on a display rack at its open front; the alley runs straight and ends at a narrow pawnshop front with an old signboard hanging crooked at one end; frost on the shaded ground; just outside the alley mouth on the main street (keep consistent with the reference image renders/sets/market_alley.png)
[CHAR] Seonyoung, 23-year-old Korean woman, 162cm, slim, round face, voluminous softly permed 1980s bob with fluffy bangs, large round thin gold-rim glasses; outfit in this scene: a beige duffle coat and a scarf over a long skirt
[CHAR] an elderly Korean pawnbroker with a magnifying loupe pushed up on his forehead, a dark vest over a white shirt, sleeve garters
[PROP] an old shop sign board, a long horizontal sign board with blank letter plates, one plate hanging crooked from a single nail, painted metal, 300 by 60cm, peeling paint, rust (match the reference image renders/props/pawnshop_sign.png)
[PROP] an empty black violin case, a shaped violin case wide at one end and narrow at the other, two metal latches and a leather handle (match the reference image renders/props/empty_violin_case__s3_pawnshop.png)
[PROP] a few sheets of printed sheet music, loose sheets with staff lines and dense note marks that are not readable, paper, 25.7 by 36.4cm, curled corners, fold creases (match the reference image renders/props/sheet_music.png)

## 2-035 — S10 국밥집 천막 (낮, 1987년 11월 말, 비)
- 크기 800×1600 · MS · 아이레벨 · 은주(중), 덕수(좌), 동민(우), 미자(우·전경)

```

[SET] Seen from the front entrance flap looking in (facing north): a long tent about 5 m wide and 8 m deep on a beaten-earth floor, patched beige and khaki canvas on a wooden pole frame, one tall wooden center pole halfway in with a bare bulb on a cord and a paper calendar nailed to it; at the far back the huge iron soup pot steaming on a brick coal fire with a small worktable and stacked bowls beside it, and to the right of the pot a rear flap that can be tied open; the benches are wide low wooden platforms (pyeongsang) with long low tables on them: two along the left (west) side and one long one along the right (east) side; the right side wall flap is rolled up, showing the corrugated-iron junk shop 3 m away; an open earth floor down the middle in front of the platforms. [CHAR] Deoksu, 12-year-old Korean boy, 155cm, big and chubby, round face, short crew cut (no fade), droopy gentle eyes, shirt buttons always done up one hole off; outfit in this scene: a blue plaid shirt over a stretched, slightly yellowed thin white cotton-knit sleeveless undershirt (a Korean 'running shirt', not an athletic tank top)

## 2-054 — S15 국밥집 천막 (저녁, 1988년 1월)

## 2-083 — S25 은주네 판잣집, 쪽방 (밤)

## 2-088 — S28 곽 영감 고물상 앞 (깊은 밤)
